  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Player 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - PlayerID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      
      ## Policies
      - Spaceflight Pioneers (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Spaceflight Pioneers: Gain a free Great Engineer and Great Scientist. May finish Spaceship parts with Great Engineers, and expending a Great Person grants 100 Science, scaling with Era. Hurrying Production with Great Engineers 50% more effective.
      - Socialist Realism (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Socialist Realism: Unlocks the Propaganda Process (converts 15% of Production into Tourism). +25% Tourism to other Civilizations following Order. +4 Production from all Great Works.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Iron Curtain (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Iron Curtain: Free Courthouse upon immediate City annexation. +200% Food or Production from Internal Trade Routes. City connections generate +5 Gold and Production.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 10
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 8
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 9
        - WarBias: 9
        - HostileBias: 9
        - WarmongerHate: 10
        - NeutralBias: 1
        - FriendlyBias: 9
        - GuardedBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 1
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Friendliness: 10
        - WorkWithWillingness: 10
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 10
        - Loyalty: 10
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 1
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 1
        - MinorCivWarBias: 1
        - DenounceWillingness: 10
        - Forgiveness: 1
        - Meanness: 8
        - Neediness: 9
        - Chattiness: 9
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: India continues its overwhelming military build-up and vassalization of Sweden. Increase WarBias and HostileBias to ensure our AI treats India with maximum aggression and rejects peace. Increase Neediness to reflect urgent requirement for allied support from Brazil and Mongolia against the Indian coalition. This is a total war scenario, requiring maximal focus on military coordination and hostility towards the enemy coalition.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Radar
        - Rationale: India's nuclear strike on Honolulu and continued air/naval superiority demands immediate air power. Radar unlocks Fighter and Heavy Bomber for air defense and offensive strikes. Telecommunications can wait; survival requires modern air units to counter Indian carriers, B17s, and XCOM squads.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Double Agents (Policy)
        - Rationale: India's espionage activity remains high. This policy (available in 10 turns) provides 200 Spy Points and Science when identifying foreign spies, enhancing our counterintelligence to protect our investments and yields. Synergizes with our max Espionage flavor. Given the intense conflict, denying intelligence to India and gaining Science is more critical than short-term economic or military policies.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### The Celts
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: The Celts are India's vassal and at war with us. Keeping private hostility at -100 ensures our AI treats them as an extension of India's coalition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 286
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: Polynesia is at war with us, has denounced us, and is firmly aligned with India via defensive pacts. Their navy is active near our coastal zones. Keeping private relationship at -80 ensures our AI prioritizes military actions against them, focuses on naval and land engagements, and resists peace overtures unless strategically advantageous.
        - UpdatedTurn: 295
        
        ### Kwa-Bulawayo
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: Kwa-Bulawayo is under direct naval assault from India (suffering city bombardment). Increasing private relationship to 25 (from 20) further encourages our AI to prioritize naval and military actions to liberate and defend this critical city-state. This adjustment signals urgency for unit gifts, naval support, and liberation efforts to secure our alliance and deny India influence.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Jakarta
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Jakarta is at war with us and a key Indian ally. We need to apply military pressure to defend ourselves and eventually flip it. Private -50 signals moderate hostility.
        - UpdatedTurn: 312
        
        ### Warsaw
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Warsaw has declared war on us and is strategically located near our core cities. Maintaining maximum private hostility ensures our AI prioritizes capturing or neutralizing this city-state to secure our borders and deny our enemies a forward base.
        - UpdatedTurn: 324
        
        ### Zurich
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: Zurich is our sphere of influence city-state and under direct threat from Germany and Sweden. Increasing private relationship to 70 ensures our AI strongly defends Zurich, provides military support, and maintains our alliance. This is critical for retaining delegates and securing our core territory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 327
        
        ### Germany
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Germany remains at war with us, allied with Sweden. Their city Dortmund is under siege. Maintain maximum hostility to ensure continued naval bombardment and eventual capture of Dortmund to secure western flank.
        - UpdatedTurn: 341
        
        ### Sweden
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Sweden is actively proposing Decolonization in World Congress (next turn) which would harm our city-state alliances. They are at war with us, allied with Germany and Polynesia, and have 12 delegates (most). Maximum hostility ensures we resist their diplomatic maneuvers and focus military efforts on them. Critical to maintain pressure to reduce their influence.
        - UpdatedTurn: 344
        
        ### India
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: India continues existential threat: they built more Fleet Destroyers, a B17 (upgraded Heavy Bomber), XCOM Squad, and used an Atomic Bomb on Cardiff (Swedish city). Their naval aggression destroyed our Destroyer. We must maintain maximum hostility (-100) to ensure our AI prioritizes defense, rejects peace, and focuses on countering Indian advances. Their tech lead and nuclear capability demand unwavering opposition.
        - UpdatedTurn: 345
        
        ### Antwerp
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Antwerp (City-State ID 30) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and prioritizes military actions against it if it becomes a threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Dorestad
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Dorestad (City-State ID 34) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and focuses on neutralizing it.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Trowulan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Trowulan (City-State ID 37) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy and resists any peace overtures.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Msoura
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Msoura (City-State ID 26) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI prioritizes military actions against it.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Brussels
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Brussels (City-State ID 27) has declared war on us and is an Indian ally. Set private relationship to -100 to ensure our AI treats it as an enemy.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### Mongolia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Mongolia remains our crucial defensive pact ally and Declaration of Friend. They are engaging India on the northern front. We just renewed an Open Borders deal with them (Turn 347). Maintaining maximum private relationship (100) ensures our AI prioritizes military coordination, joint naval operations, and encourages them to pressure Sweden's northern cities (Turfan, Hsia). Their 4 delegates are vital for World Congress votes against Sweden's Decolonization proposal next turn. We may gift them gold or units to offset their combat penalty unhappiness.
        - UpdatedTurn: 347
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Brazil is our other critical defensive pact ally. They recaptured Belo Horizonte from India and are actively building naval units (Cruiser, Light Tank). Their military is engaged near Aztlan and our western sea zones. Maximum private relationship (100) ensures our AI coordinates closely with Brazil, shares intelligence, and prioritizes joint naval strikes against Indian/German fleets. Brazil's 8 delegates are essential for World Congress voting. We must strengthen this alliance to withstand India's onslaught.
        - UpdatedTurn: 347
        
        ### Aztlan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Aztlan is now our allied city-state (re-allied via Great Diplomat). Set private relationship to 100 to ensure our AI prioritizes its defense, sends unit gifts, and maintains the alliance. This secures our western flank and denies enemies a base.
        - UpdatedTurn: 349
        
        ### Cahokia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Cahokia is now our allied city-state (allied via influence). Previously set to -100 when enemy, now adjust to 100 to encourage military support and defense. This helps secure our northern border against Sweden and India.
        - UpdatedTurn: 349
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: India's nuclear escalation and overwhelming military require maximum defensive posture. Increase Defense to 100 for stronger city garrisons and fortifications. MilitaryTraining to 85 for better unit quality via promotions and upgrades. Recon to 70 for improved scouting of Indian movements. UseNuke to 95 to reflect readiness for nuclear retaliation if attacked with WMDs. Maintain high Air, Antiair, Mobilization, and CityDefense to withstand Indian assaults.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 43
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 1 turn
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Travel Ban:
            - Description: Restricts international travel between Civilizations. Reduces the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 35%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Babylonian Sphere of Influence: Zurich:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Buddhism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:

            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.

        You, Nebuchadnezzar II (leader of Babylon, Player 6), are making strategic decisions after turn 353.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Nebuchadnezzar II, leader of Babylon (Opponent 6).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Babylon
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Bowman, Replacing Archer (Requires technology: Calendar)
        - Unique Unit: Sabum Kibitum, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Walls of Babylon, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Unique Building: Etemenanki, Replacing National Monument (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: Receive a Free Great Scientist when you discover Writing, and Great Scientists are earned 50% faster than normal. Investing Gold in Buildings reduces their Production cost by an additional 15%.
      - Leader: Nebuchadnezzar II
      - OpponentID: 6
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Satellites: Allow you to build the Apollo Program, a project necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: The Internet, Lasers
      - Radar: Allows you to build the Heavy Bomber, a deadly air Unit capable of delivering death from afar, and the Fighter, an air Unit designed to wrest control of the skies from enemy aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Advanced Ballistics, Mobile Tactics
      - Alternative Energy: Allows you to build the Solar Plant, a building which may only be built in Cities on or next to Desert, and increases Production. Also allows you to build the Recycling Center, which provides bonus Aluminum.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics
      
      ## Policies
      - Spaceflight Pioneers (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Spaceflight Pioneers: Gain a free Great Engineer and Great Scientist. May finish Spaceship parts with Great Engineers, and expending a Great Person grants 100 Science, scaling with Era. Hurrying Production with Great Engineers 50% more effective.
      - Socialist Realism (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Socialist Realism: Unlocks the Propaganda Process (converts 15% of Production into Tourism). +25% Tourism to other Civilizations following Order. +4 Production from all Great Works.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Iron Curtain (Level 3 Tenet of Ideology Order): Iron Curtain: Free Courthouse upon immediate City annexation. +200% Food or Production from Internal Trade Routes. City connections generate +5 Gold and Production.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exchange Markets (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +1 Trade Route.
        - * +1 Happiness for every active Trade Route.
        - * +15% Tourism modifier for Trade Routes.
        - * Resources from City-States count towards Global Monopolies.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
        - Flavors:
          - Culture: 100
      - rationale: India's impending Cultural Victory requires us to maximize our Culture output. Increasing our Culture flavor to 100 will prioritize culture-generating infrastructure, which will boost our culture per turn and help push India's influence over us back below 100%, effectively blocking their path to a Cultural Victory. We maintain our United Nations grand strategy to secure diplomatic dominance and block India in the World Congress.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: With the peace treaty with Germany expiring in 2 turns and a massive defensive war against India and Sweden ongoing, Patriotic War (+20% combat strength for land units near cities) is critical. It will secure our frontline cities like Dortmund, Warsaw, and Opis, and provide the tactical edge needed to defend our territory and push into Sweden's Uppsala.
        - Policy: Patriotic War
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: Radar is our highest defense priority. India's carrier-based air power and B17 bombers pose an existential threat. Unlocking Radar allows us to upgrade our Triplanes and Bombers to Fighters and Heavy Bombers, ensuring we can intercept Indian air strikes and protect our coasts.
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Polynesia has lost its capital, Honolulu, to India and is under severe distress. They are a natural ally against the Indian hegemon. Adjusting relationship to 50 to encourage peace and coordinate defensive efforts against our common enemy.
        - Private: 50
        - TargetID: 5
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Cahokia is allied with Germany and is situated right on our northern/western flank. If Germany declares war in 2 turns, Cahokia will join them and threaten our territory. Set to -100 to prepare our forces for immediate defensive and neutralizing actions.
        - Private: -100
        - TargetID: 35
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Oviedo is allied with Sweden and is currently at war with us. Adjusting to -100 ensures our AI treats them as an active threat and focuses on neutralizing their units.
        - TargetID: 22
        - Private: -100
    - #6:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 32
        - Rationale: Zurich is our permanent ally and a crucial buffer zone. Increasing to 100 ensures maximum defense, support, and resource gifts to keep them secure against enemy advances.
    - #7:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Kyiv is allied with Germany. In the event of a German war, Kyiv will become an enemy. Setting relationship to -50 to cautiously prepare for defensive actions.
        - TargetID: 23
        - Private: -50
  - tokens:

    - reasoningTokens: 14380
    - outputTokens: 541
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Current Status**
            
            My current focus is on analyzing our position at turn 353. We're nearing Telecommunications research, and I'm reviewing our gold reserves and upcoming policy choices.
            
            **Evaluating World Congress**
            
            My attention is now on the impending World Congress session. Several key resolutions are up for vote, including repealing Casus Belli and Travel Ban, and enacting a German Sphere of Influence. I am assessing the implications of these, particularly in relation to our alliances and rivalries.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Shifts**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our geopolitical standing given recent events. India's capture of Polynesia's capital has destabilized the region, and I'm assessing the ripple effects, particularly concerning our ongoing conflicts and potential alliance realignments as a result of this power shift.
            
            **Confirming Engagements**
            
            I'm clarifying our active conflicts. It appears we are at war with India, Sweden, and the Celts, and my review shows an impending expiration of our peace treaty with Germany in two turns, a crucial detail given their alliance with Sweden.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Landscape**
            
            I'm revising our understanding of the global power dynamics. It's become clear that India, as Sweden's and the Celts' overlord, represents a monumental threat with its overwhelming military strength and significant influence in the World Congress. This strategic realization demands immediate reconsideration of our current conflicts.
            
            **Analyzing Combat Outcomes**
            
            My current focus is on the recent naval engagements. It seems India's Fleet Destroyer sustained significant damage, and I'm reviewing the logs to understand the full extent of its impact on the ongoing conflict. There's also a discrepancy in our research direction that needs immediate clarification.
            
            **Clarifying Research Direction**
            
            I'm re-examining our research queue. It appears Telecommunications is underway, which is concerning as Radar isn't yet complete, and Radar is available to research. I need to understand why Telecommunications was prioritized.
            
            **Prioritizing Research**
            
            I'm confirming that Telecommunications will complete in 7 turns, followed by Radar, which is ideal for countering India's air power. Our current research system doesn't allow for mid-research changes. I'm now reviewing policy options, noting that our next policy change is scheduled in 8 turns.
            
            **Clarifying Policy Options**
            
            I'm reviewing our Order policies and have identified a discrepancy in the listed levels for "Dictatorship of the Proletariat." It appears "Double Agents" has been selected for our next policy change, and I'm evaluating its impact along with other available Tenets.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Choices**
            
            I am re-evaluating our current policy direction. Given the ongoing war, I'm comparing the benefits of "Double Agents" against military-focused tenets like "Patriotic War" and economic boosts from "Communism." The impact on our occupied cities and overall happiness is also a significant factor.
            
            **Exploring Tenet Requirements**
            
            I'm investigating the prerequisites for higher-tier tenets, specifically focusing on unlocking "Spaceflight Pioneers." It seems my understanding of the tenet progression, particularly regarding the number of L1 and L2 tenets needed, requires further clarification.
            
            **Unlocking Tenet Paths**
            
            I'm reassessing our tenet progression. It appears we only have one Tier 1 tenet currently, "Hero of the People," and need another to unlock Tier 3 options. I'm reviewing "Double Agents," "Communism," "People's Army," and "Patriotic War" to determine the best choice.
            
            **Defending Near Nantes**
            
            My current focus is on the defense of Nantes. We're holding the line against Indian and Celtic forces, even managing to damage their XCOM squads. Our naval presence is also bolstering our position.
            
            **Preparing for German Renewed Conflict**
            
            I'm reassessing our defensive posture regarding Germany. With their peace treaty expiring soon and their alliance with Sweden, coupled with our occupation of Dortmund, it's imperative to prepare for a potential renewed conflict. I'm evaluating our military strength and our capacity to defend our occupied territories.
            
            **Leveraging Alliances**
            
            My latest assessment confirms that our defensive pacts with Brazil and Mongolia are crucial. If Germany initiates hostilities, they will join us against them and Sweden. This is particularly advantageous as India is already engaged in war with Brazil and Mongolia.
            
            **Analyzing Alliance Dynamics**
            
            I am reviewing Germany's relationships and potential conflict triggers. Despite peace treaties, their denunciations of us, Brazil, and Mongolia, alongside Sweden's existing war with us, create a complex scenario. Germany may join Sweden against us, or vice-versa.
            
            **Analyzing City-State Engagements**
            
            I'm reviewing our standing with allied city-states. Zurich and Aztlan are both at war with our current adversaries and are heavily fortified. Notably, Aztlan recently received a Battleship, bolstering their defensive capabilities.
            
            **Revising City-State Allegiances**
            
            I've discovered a critical miscalculation regarding Cahokia. It's not our allied city-state but a major ally of Germany. This revelation necessitates a complete reevaluation of our diplomatic approach to them, as they could pose a threat.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            I've realized Cahokia is actually allied with Germany, not us, and is geographically positioned to aid them. This critical piece of information requires a complete shift in our approach to them. Additionally, I'm investigating Kyiv's situation and the World Congress vote on their allegiance to Germany, recognizing India's decisive influence on the outcome.
            
            **Opposing Travel Ban Repeal**
            
            I've identified an urgent threat: India is on the cusp of a Cultural Victory, needing only to become influential over Brazil. Repealing the Travel Ban would significantly boost their tourism. My immediate priority is to ensure we vote NO on this repeal, dedicating all our votes to this effort.
            
            **Voting Against India**
            
            My current focus is on thwarting India's Cultural Victory. They need to be influential over Brazil, and repealing the Travel Ban aids this. I'm dedicating all our votes to opposing this repeal, as India's tourism output is alarmingly high.
            
            **Capturing Key Cities**
            
            I've confirmed the successful occupation of Hyderabad and Madurai from India, a significant strategic win. However, Madurai's current defense seems weak, prompting me to analyze potential Indian counter-attacks from their eastern cities. I'm scrutinizing our coordinates for both occupied and enemy territories.
            
            **Securing New Acquisitions**
            
            I'm confirming the locations of our recently captured cities, Hyderabad and Madurai. It's becoming clear they are situated near the Mongolian border, but initial scans indicate no immediate enemy units or sea threats in their vicinity, suggesting a stable northern front for now.
            
            **Defending Near Nantes**
            
            My current focus is on the defense of Nantes, which is held by the Celts, a vassal of India. We're facing a formidable Indian and Celtic presence, including multiple XCOM squads and Machine Guns. Our naval strength is aiding our position, and I'm assessing our closest city, Opis, which is only 8 tiles away and a potential reinforcement point.
            
            **Reinforcing Strategic Positions**
            
            I'm focusing on fortifying Opis and Shushan due to their strong defensive capabilities. However, a critical oversight regarding Sweden's proximity to Warsaw and Dortmund necessitates an immediate reassessment of our troop deployments. Their city, Uppsala, poses an imminent threat, requiring urgent attention before it can advance.
            
            **Assessing Aerial Threats**
            
            My latest analysis reveals India possesses a highly advanced B17 bomber, significantly concerning given its promotions and proximity. However, upon calculating distances, its effective range does not reach our key eastern cities like Dortmund or Warsaw, mitigating the immediate aerial threat from that specific unit.
            
            **Defending Coastal Cities**
            
            I'm reassessing our air defense capabilities. While India's B17 bombers can't reach our eastern cities directly from Delhi, their carriers pose a significant threat to our coastal defenses. Fortunately, we have Triplanes and Anti-Aircraft Guns positioned in key eastern cities to intercept potential strikes.
            
            **Analyzing City Production**
            
            I'm reviewing our city production queues. It's concerning that several cities are locked into the ISS infinite project, which requires World Congress. I need to understand why this happened and if it can be overridden.
            
            **Optimizing Production Queues**
            
            I'm confirming that the ISS and Public Works projects are intentionally active. Given our limited military supply, prioritizing science and infrastructure via these projects is a smart tactical decision to maximize our advantage without penalties.
            
            **Prioritizing Satellite Research**
            
            I'm reconfirming our research path. Telecommunications is finishing, and while Radar is available, Satellites are the immediate priority to counter India's Science Victory progress. We're second in score and science output, making this crucial.
            
            **Re-evaluating Research Priority**
            
            I'm reassessing our research path given the discovery of Satellites as an option. While Radar is crucial for air defense against India's bombers, India has already completed Apollo. Therefore, prioritizing Satellites immediately after Telecommunications is essential for us to pursue our own Science Victory and counter India's lead.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Upgrades**
            
            I'm weighing the immediate defensive benefits of Radar against the long-term Science Victory race. Upgrading our existing air units with Radar is appealing given India's bomber threat, especially since it doesn't increase our unit cap. However, India's Science Victory progress, evidenced by their Apollo completion, necessitates prioritizing Satellites immediately after Telecommunications to maintain our own path to victory and counter their lead.
            
            **Launching Offensive Strike**
            
            My current focus is on capturing Uppsala from Sweden. The defenses are surprisingly weak, and we have significant offensive power in nearby cities. This presents a prime opportunity to weaken India's vassal, expand our territory, and gain valuable delegates, all without directly engaging India's main forces.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm reviewing our relationships, noting the Celts are an Indian vassal and at war. Intriguingly, Polynesia, recently denounced by us, is now at war with India. Given this shift, I'm reconsidering our negative private stance towards them, as they are now a potential ally against our primary foe.
            
            **Preventing German Hostilities**
            
            I'm now focused on deterring Germany from declaring war. Given their substantial military and alliance with Sweden, their hostility would severely disrupt our war with India. I'm exploring ways to improve our private relationship with them, though their extreme negative opinion and recent denunciations present a significant challenge.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Alliances**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our relationships with Germany, Brazil, Mongolia, and Cahokia. My focus is on setting optimal private diplomacy scores to prepare for German aggression and leverage our alliances against India, ensuring defensive garrisons are in place despite their potential alliance shifts.
            
            **Adjusting City-State Diplomacy**
            
            I'm setting Cahokia to a significantly negative relationship to prepare for potential German conflict. Zurich and Aztlan will maintain high positive standings due to our alliances and sphere of influence. I'm also ensuring city-states allied with our enemies, like Oviedo and those in India's sphere, are set to maximum negativity.
            
            **Prioritizing Radar Research**
            
            My current focus is on research. While Satellites seem important for the Science Victory race, Radar's immediate defensive benefits against India's bombers are critical, especially with our existing air units. I believe upgrading our current air fleet will provide a more significant tactical advantage right now.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Research**
            
            I'm prioritizing Radar research immediately. India's bomber threat necessitates this defensive upgrade for our existing air units. I'm also reconsidering our next policy change, leaning towards "Patriotic War" for its significant combat bonuses and T-34 production capabilities, which would greatly aid our current conflicts.
            
            **Selecting Policy Upgrade**
            
            I am now choosing our next policy. Given our ongoing wars with India, Sweden, and the Celts, and the looming threat of Germany, "Patriotic War" offers critical combat bonuses and T-34 production, making it the essential choice for survival and defending our cities.
            
            **Updating Relationships**
            
            I'm adjusting our diplomatic standing. Polynesia, now at war with India, is a potential ally. I'm setting our private relationship with them to positive, while severing ties with Cahokia, Germany's ally, to prepare for potential conflict.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Leverage**
            
            I'm refining our relationships, shifting Polynesia to a potential ally given their war with India, and actively distancing ourselves from Cahokia due to their German alliance and strategic positioning. My focus remains on solidifying alliances with Brazil and Mongolia while deterring Germany.
            
            **Refining Diplomatic Leverage**
            
            I'm refining our relationships, shifting Polynesia to a potential ally given their war with India, and actively distancing ourselves from Cahokia due to their German alliance and strategic positioning. My focus remains on solidifying alliances with Brazil and Mongolia while deterring Germany. I will also set Oviedo to a negative relationship, as they are allied with Sweden and currently at war with us. Zurich will remain a positive permanent ally to ensure maximum support.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Priorities**
            
            I am re-evaluating our strategic flavors. While our current balance is effective, India's tourism presents a growing challenge. I am considering increasing our Culture flavor from 75 to 90 to bolster our cultural output and resist their influence, even though they are already influential over us.
            
            **Boosting Culture Output**
            
            My current focus is on countering India's looming Cultural Victory. I've realized that by significantly increasing our Culture flavor, we can potentially lower their influence over us from "Influential" back to "Popular," thus blocking their victory condition. This requires a substantial increase in our cultural output.
            
            **Prioritizing Global Influence**
            
            I'm focusing on maximizing our World Congress influence. With all other flavors at 100, I'm setting Culture to 100 as well. My intention is to leverage our diplomatic strength and allied city-states towards a Diplomatic Victory via "United Nations," effectively blocking India's progress.
            
            **Finalizing Diplomatic Engagements**
            
            I am finalizing our diplomatic stances. Polynesia will become a positive ally against India, while Cahokia, allied with Germany, will be set to maximum negativity to prepare for conflict. Zurich will remain a strong ally, and Oviedo will be set to negative due to its alliance with Sweden. This ensures our defensive and offensive capabilities are optimized.
            
            **Finalizing Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I'm adjusting our city-state relationships for maximum strategic advantage. Cahokia and Oviedo are now set to negative due to their alliances with our adversaries. Kyiv will also see a negative adjustment due to its German ties. Zurich remains a steadfast ally. I'm also confirming Germany's relationship is already at its lowest.
            
            **Finalizing Strategic Directives**
            
            I'm consolidating our immediate actions into clear directives. This involves setting our Grand Strategy to "United Nations" and maximizing Culture flavor, implementing "Patriotic War" as our next policy, and prioritizing Radar research. I'm also finalizing diplomatic shifts for key city-states and Polynesia to optimize our alliances and prepare for potential German hostilities.

          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: With the peace treaty with Germany expiring in 2 turns and a massive defensive war against India and Sweden ongoing, Patriotic War (+20% combat strength for land units near cities) is critical.             - Policy: Patriotic War
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined

          - toolCallId: OagvL8DDFyJKxhX3

Unlocking Radar allows us to upgrade our Triplanes and Bombers to Fighters and Heavy Bombers, ensuring we can intercept Indian air strikes and protect our coasts.
